    <script>
    (function () {
      function disableTurboDrive() {
        try {
          if (window.Turbo && window.Turbo.session) {
            window.Turbo.session.drive = false;
            document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-turbo-prefetch', 'false');
            console.log('Turbo Drive disabled after Donorbox runtime load.');
            return true;
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('Unable to disable Turbo Drive:', e);
        }
        return false;
      }

      if (disableTurboDrive()) return;

      var tries = 0;
      var maxTries = 100;
      var timer = setInterval(function () {
        tries++;
        if (disableTurboDrive() || tries >= maxTries) {
          clearInterval(timer);
        }
      }, 100);
    })();
    </script>

<div id="text-block-6a9c55ffb9fa7" class="mk-text-block  jupiter-donut- ">

	
	<h3>BUILDING STRONG MUSLIM FOUNDATIONS — CHARACTER, KNOWLEDGE &amp; QUR’AN</h3>

	<div class="clearboth"></div>
</div>

	</div>
<style id="mk-shortcode-style-6a9c55ffba15f" type="text/css">#text-block-6a9c55ffba15f { margin-bottom:0px; text-align:left; }</style>	<div class=" vc_custom_1788320724317">

<div id="text-block-6a9c55ffba15f" class="mk-text-block  mk-animate-element left-to-right  jupiter-donut- ">

	
	<section id="isPasted" dir="auto" data-turn-id="request-WEB:e1241b43-38a1-461a-a57e-8736fcaa0f37-1" data-testid="conversation-turn-4" data-scroll-anchor="false" data-turn="assistant">
<div dir="auto" tabindex="0" data-message-author-role="assistant" data-message-id="525718f1-0bba-4dc9-99a7-11602bf89bd1" data-message-model-slug="gpt-5-5" data-turn-start-message="true">
<p>Give your child a meaningful Islamic education designed to nurture faith, character, knowledge, and a lifelong connection with the Qur’an. Ihsan Academy offers multiple learning paths, including Foundations, Nooraniyah, Hafith, Arabic Studies, and Special Needs, with programs available at both ICPC-Paterson and ICPC-Clifton.</p>
<p>Students can choose the path that best supports their growth while building a strong Muslim identity in a welcoming and structured environment.</p>
</div>
</section>

	<div class="clearboth"></div>
</div>

	var slideIndex = 1;
	showSlides_new(slideIndex);

	function plusSlides(n) {
	  showSlides_new(slideIndex += n);
	}

	function currentSlide_new(n) {
	  showSlides_new(slideIndex = n);
	}

	function showSlides_new(n) {
	  var i;
	  var slides = document.getElementsByClassName("mySlides_new");
	  var dots = document.getElementsByClassName("dots");
	  if (n > slides.length) {slideIndex = 1}    
	  if (n < 1) {slideIndex = slides.length}
	  for (i = 0; i < slides.length; i++) {
		  slides[i].style.display = "none";  
	  }
	 for (i = 0; i < dots.length; i++) {
		  dots[i].className = dots[i].className.replace(" active", "");
	  }
	  slides[slideIndex-1].style.display = "block";  
	  dots[slideIndex-1].className += " active";
	}

						const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
							entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
								if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
									let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
									if( lazyloadBackground ) {
										lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
									}
									lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
								}
							});
						}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
						lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
							lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
						} );
					};
					const events = [
						'DOMContentLoaded',
						'elementor/lazyload/observe',
					];
					events.forEach( ( event ) => {
						document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
					} );
				} )();
			</script>
